## Brazil added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Brazil's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDF — **1 book, a pupil textbook: *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio* (SEED-PR), the Paraná state public textbook for Ensino Médio (Grades 10–12), in Portuguese. There is no teacher guide in the Brazil Maths set in hand, so every Brazil row in this course is a pupil-textbook row — there are no teacher-evidence rows, and Brazil counts in every lesson it stocks** — and placed 128 teaching items. The expansion map's “1 student + 0 teacher = 1” is correct: books.json (grade-10/gov/brazil) and textbooks.tsv each carry exactly one Brazil Mathematics entry, and phase3-push-state.json lists the file as pushed. The staging folder for the book is empty, so there was no local copy to read, and the authenticated GitHub asset route failed in this pass (the mid-size-books release's asset listing repeatedly disconnected through the proxy, and the asset's download URL answered 404) — the GitHub copy's existence and size were therefore NOT verified here, and a library-side check of that asset is owed (a route failure, not a negative finding). The book was instead read from its own recorded source_url — the Wayback capture banked by the 2026-10-22 CDX prep (capture 20240421222141) — which landed at exactly the prep's expected 6,379,928 B, with 240 pages and the correct cover and catalogue block; the book read is certainly this book. Printed page = PDF page throughout. Four book errors found in the read (placements E1–E4) are carried corrected on the rows they touch — in the sequences-and-series (E1), ratio-and-proportion (E2), coordinate-geometry (E3), barycentres (E4) and loci (E4) lessons; two further misprints are recorded, not taught (R5: the risk exponent typeset once as 0.2525 for 0.2528, printed p. 58 — noted on the item 28 row in the logarithms-and-exponentials lesson; R6: glucose printed “C6A12O6”, printed p. 35, a chemistry misprint), and the Aristarchus bound passage (printed p. 169, record item R7) could not be confirmed either way as extracted and is cited nowhere. The placements' named exclusions — the four section introductions and the general presentation (framing and history), the context essays and debates that carry no new maths, the biographical and historical passages except where the passage itself teaches a method, the “Obras Consultadas” reference lists after every chapter, and the blank “Anotações” pages — are stocked nowhere. Into this lesson the placements stock 13 items (all from the pupil textbook) from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio* (pupil textbook, Ensino Médio — Grades 10–12) — Brazil, Secretaria de Estado da Educação do Paraná (SEED-PR), Curitiba, 2nd edition 2006, ISBN 85-85380-39-X; 6,379,928 bytes, 240 PDF pp., MD5 e3dac71a6d075dd142282376da6fa785. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Read from the banked Wayback capture of the book's recorded source_url at the exact recorded size; the GitHub release-asset copy could not be verified in this pass (route failure — a library-side asset check is owed). The catalogue block's “p. 216” is the 1st-edition pagination and does not match this file's 240 pages (record item R1); all citations use the actual printed folios. Folha 6's chapter heading (printed p. 77) adds “Corrupção & Política –” to the contents-list title (record item R2) — the same chapter. Folhas 9 and 10 are filed by the book inside its Funções section although their content is trigonometry (record item R3); the placements follow the content.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| The lottery's chances stated as counted ratios: a minimum bet's chance is 1 in 2,332 for the quadra, 1 in 154,518 for the quina and 1 in 50,063,860 for the sena | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed p. 220 |
| Probability defined: from the table, about 41 of every 100 Saveiro cars in a region may have a claim, so the probability of a claim is 41/100 = 0.41 = 41% — the probability of an event is the ratio between the number of favourable possibilities and the total number of possibilities | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed p. 225 |
| The sample space and the event named: all the Saveiros in the situation are the “Espaço Amostral” (sample space); one Saveiro's involvement in a claim is the “Evento” (event) | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed p. 225 |
| The random experiment: which cars will actually have claims cannot be known for certain — a situation described in terms of chances is an “Experimento Aleatório” | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed p. 225 |
| The effect of sample size on probability: 250 or more girls among the first 500 babies at a large maternity hospital against 25 or more among the first 50 at a small one — the two facts are not equally likely; the small-sample fact is the more probable | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed p. 226 |
| The fair (“honest”) die: a symmetric die gives every face the same chance; a die unbalanced by extra material does not | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed pp. 227–228 |
| Dice sample spaces and single-face probabilities: the sample space of one fair die and of two dice, and the probability of the event 1, and of the event 6, in one throw | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed p. 228 |
| The Chevalier de Méré problem: with a pair of honest dice, how many throws are needed for a favourable (greater than 50%) chance of getting a double-six at least once | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed pp. 228–229 |
| Unequal chances in a raffle: drawing a paper numbered n gives a pupil n coupons, so the pupils do NOT all have the same probability of winning the calculator — and strategies that would give everyone the same probability | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed p. 229 |
| Judging a dice game's payouts by their probabilities with a fair die: losing on 4, 5 or 6 (probability 3/6), winning 50% extra on 2 or 3 (2/6), and winning three times the stake on 1 (1/6) | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed p. 230 |
| Recomputing those chances for a loaded die in which 6 comes up three times as often as each other number — every other face keeping the same probability — and judging whether the game is balanced, and for whom | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed p. 230 |
| Testing a parity game for fairness: in “par ou ímpar” (odds and evens) with the closed hand (zero) forbidden, the losing (odd) team claims the game is unjust — pupils build the scheme of possible finger counts and check whether par and ímpar have different probabilities | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed p. 231 |
| The bounds of probability and its extreme cases: a probability P satisfies 0 ≤ P ≤ 1 (0% to 100%); a DNA test with no matching alleles gives probability 0% of paternity — an impossible event — while a true match gives ≈ 99.99…% | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed pp. 232–233 |

Brazil is added to the countries line and to the acceptance count above on its pupil-textbook rows — the one book in its set is a pupil textbook and there are no teacher-evidence rows. Brazil's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (the 1) has been read and stocked.

## Bhutan added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Bhutan's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **16 files: 13 student books and 3 teacher's guides of the Royal Government of Bhutan's *Understanding Mathematics* series (Department of School Education / Ministry of Education and Skills Development), English-medium throughout** — and placed 657 pupil teaching items and 30 teacher-evidence items. **Set correction, proved by the read: the expansion map's count of 10 student + 3 teacher is WRONG — the map counted only the textbooks.tsv snapshot (PP, I, II, IV–X) and missed the Class III, XI and XII student books recorded as secured and pushed in the staging books.json files and the push state; the true in-hand set is 13 student + 3 teacher = 16 files, every one grabbed and verified (byte size on every file; MD5 against books.json on the 11 files that carry one — all match; page counts match every record) and read in full.** The Class I staging-path collision is resolved: the main-branch grade-01 file is the student activity book (Book 3); the Class I teacher's guide (Book 4) is a separate, MD5-verified file that books.json records under the same staging path name. **Critical filing flag, carried exactly: both BHSEC filings claim "complete book, not a sample/preview" — FALSE. The Class XI file holds only chapters 1–5 of the 26 its own contents lists and the Class XII file only chapters 1–4 of the 19 its contents lists; only the chapters actually present were placed, and both books are cited as the partial excerpts they are.** The 3 teacher's guides are teacher evidence only: their items are named below as teacher evidence and are never counted as pupil teaching. The Class 1 activity book's Reproducible Pages (printed pp. 75–80) and Summative Assessment Recording Sheets (pp. 81–100) are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The Class 2 activity book's Summative Assessment Recording Sheets (printed pp. 99–118) are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The pupil Chapter 9 pages open at Lesson 3 (Interpreting Bar Graphs) — no pupil Lessons 1–2 for that chapter appear in the file; the guide's parallel survey and data-collection lessons stand as teacher evidence instead. Book errors in the Class 2 book are carried corrected: its imprint prints the copyright year as "20234", read as 2023 as on its guide, and its Chapter 6 Lesson 6 tells pupils to draw a cylinder and label its base and apex — a cylinder has no apex; the apex belongs to the cone in the same lesson. The Class 3 textbook's Chapter Overviews, Chapter Reviews and course-objectives/assessment front matter are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The Class IV textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. The Class V textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. The Class VI textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. Its contents numbers two different sections 6.2.2 ("Bisectors" and "EXPLORE: Sorting Quadrilaterals"); the second is cited with the duplication named. The Class VII textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. The Class VIII textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. Its contents misprints §5.3.2 (Surface Area of a Rectangular Prism) at printed p. 157, after the Unit 5 Revision at p. 150; the sequence places it at ~p. 147 and the row cites it so. The Class IX textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. The Class X textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. Its assessment introduction carries a "curriculum for Class IX" line — a carry-over in the Class X book, read as Class X. ## Canada added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Canada's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **2 works in 4 files: *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide, Elementary & Secondary* (FNESC/FNSA, ©2020), 273 pp. — the one book in Canada's classified in-hand Maths set (0 student + 1 teacher) — and the borderline pupil work *I Like Math!* (Government of Nunavut, 2017), one Level 8 guided-reading fiction book held in three language editions of 11 pp. each and filed in every record under the Inuktut Ilinniaqta Early Books label, not under Maths** — and placed 117 items (112 teacher-guide evidence items from the guide, items 1–112, plus 5 pupil items from *I Like Math!*, items S1–S5). Every item from the guide is TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE: the guide directs teachers how to teach BC mathematics through First Peoples contexts, with mini-units, activities and blackline masters, and states BC curriculum connections in its tables; it expressly does not explain the mathematics or the curriculum in detail (its printed p. 35) and presumes a trained teacher. No pupil Maths textbook is in hand for Canada: Canada has no national textbook series (education is provincial/territorial), and the five provincial student textbooks in the records (Nelson *Principles of Mathematics 10*; McGraw-Hill Ryerson *Principles of Mathematics 9*; Pearson *Math Makes Sense 3, 7, 8*) stand filled-by-exact-link only — never staged, never secured, not read here and cited nowhere (a sixth, *Nelson Mathematics 8*, is a candidate only, its imprint never read). Canada is therefore cited as EVIDENCE ONLY, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent: Canada is added to this lesson's countries line and named in its rows, but counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The *I Like Math!* items are stocked as pupil evidence from that work exactly as the placements label them — 3 carry taught content (S1, S2, S4) and 2 are context/named-only (S3, S5) — and are likewise counted in no taught-in figure; whether the Nunavut early-books titles count as pupil Maths at all is a record-side question for the parent, reported here, not ruled on. The guide's Water-Keepers items (98–105) rest on the guide's printed teaching alone: the separate FNESC Water Keepers digital resource the guide points to (printed p. 224) is in no record as in hand and was not read. The guide is the 2020 revision of the 2011 *Math First Peoples* resource; the 2011 predecessor located on a Padlet host is not a separate in-hand book and is cited nowhere. The placements' named exclusions — the guide's introduction, goals/rationale and teacher-reflection material; First Peoples Principles of Learning, story protocols and respectful-practice rules; the resource-design rubric; community-visit apparatus beyond the Interview Guide's six content categories; unit-organization front matter; the cultural/historical context narratives and stories inside the mini-units (context-setting only; no story text is reproduced anywhere in the placements or here); BLM worksheets that merely repeat an itemised activity; formative/summative assessment prompts, guiding questions, video and website lists and the Additional Resources bibliographies; and the *I Like Math!* front/back matter — teach no new mathematics and are stocked nowhere. The 5 book errors found in the read (placements E1–E5) are carried corrected on the rows they touch: E1 (sphere-volume worked example, printed p. 123 — a unit slip and a garbled intermediate line inside a volume calculation; the final answer, 500 cm³, is correct) on item 56; E2 (BLM 6 conversion sheet, printed p. 185 — the percent-to-decimal rule states the decimal-point direction backwards; both printed examples are correct) on item 83; E3 (Moose Tracks answer key, printed p. 181 — mixes “less than or equal to 20” with m < 20 and marks the boundary value inconsistently) on item 71; E4 (Circle Unit Answer Key, printed p. 211 — says “smoke hole” for “fire circle”; the project instructions at printed p. 204 state it correctly) on item 92; E5 (Weaving BLM list, printed p. 55 — numbers both Keep or Toss sheets BLM 16; they are BLM 15 and BLM 16) is a list typo affecting no item's content. Printed page = PDF page in the guide throughout; *I Like Math!* citations are its printed story folios. Canada's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 items (items 5, 96, 97, 106, 107, 110, 111 and 112).

**Book 1:** *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide, Elementary & Secondary* (teacher resource guide) — Canada, First Nations Education Steering Committee (FNESC) and First Nations Schools Association (FNSA), ©2020; 32,264,979 bytes (byte-exact against the record), 273 PDF pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide directs teachers how to teach BC mathematics through First Peoples contexts and states curriculum connections; it expressly does not explain the mathematics in detail (printed p. 35); it is not a pupil textbook.

Canada's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)** — the classified in-hand set is one teacher resource guide with no pupil Maths textbook in hand; the five exact-link provincial student textbooks were not read and are cited nowhere.

## Costa Rica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Costa Rica's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **25 books, all of them student books: the five CONED distance-secondary antologías *Antología de Matemáticas*, Niveles 7–11 (UNED/CONED — Nivel Sétimo 2017, Nivel Noveno 2018, Nivel 11 2019; Nivel Décimo by MSc. Jorge Díaz Porras) and the ITCR Proyecto EVEPRIM primary books for Grades 3 and 6 (10 textos + 10 folletos de prácticas across Relaciones y Álgebra, Números, Geometría, Medidas, Estadística and Probabilidad); 0 teacher guides for Costa Rica Maths exist in hand** — and placed 229 teaching items. **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 17 student is WRONG** — 17 is exactly the number of Costa Rica Mathematics rows in textbooks.tsv (10 EVEPRIM textos + only 2 of the 10 EVEPRIM folletos + 5 CONED antologías); the TSV was never updated for 8 folletos staged on 2026-10-25, and the staging books.json files and phase3-push-state.json agree on the true set of 25 (the folletos count student-side, per the map's own stated basis). All 25 were grabbed, verified (byte size + MD5 against books.json; the two Relaciones y Álgebra textos, which have no books.json entry, verified by size + title page as the main-repo files) and read in full — no partial excerpts, no duplicates (the SharePoint folder copies of the six texto books matched the staged sizes byte-for-byte, so they are the same files, cited once) and no unopenable files. The three Grade 3 textos were read in their November 2026 editions, the in-hand editions per books.json; the GitHub release mid-size-books-3 still holds the superseded October editions of those three textos (a record-level flag, reported, not fixed — same books, older exports; only the November editions are cited here). Two chapter promises in the CONED books are NOT teaching and place no item anywhere: CONED 7's Capítulo 4 is titled Estadística y Probabilidad but teaches statistics only (probability appears only in its introductory discussion questions, pp. 209–214), and CONED 10's Capítulo 1 objectives promise plane transformations that no Tema in the chapter teaches (only a recall of Grade 8 homothety). The placements' named exclusions — front matter, indices, bibliographies and answer keys (soluciones); the EVEPRIM answer-key chapters and the review chapters that re-teach already-placed sections; the CONED chapter motto/introduction pages and “desafío matemático” pages; and the videos and web interactives the EVEPRIM books link to — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The 5 CONED book-error groups found in the read — CONED 7: the angle table (p. 106) labels a 360° angle “CONVEXO”, the inverse-proportionality example (pp. 201–203) states v·t = 247.5 for a product of 330 and “very close to 3 hours” at 78 km/h for ≈4.23 h with garbled fraction work, the triangle inequality (p. 125) omits “any two” sides, the median answer (p. 238) gives 68 for a median of 67, the worked powers page (p. 64) misaligns its results, and a tiling exercise (p. 35) reads “largo por largo”; CONED 8: the table of contents numbers the equations Tema “Tema 4” while the body heads it “Tema 5” (numbering slip only); CONED 9: the SI prefix table (p. 74) gives exa the symbol “R” (exa is E), the radian-to-degree rule (p. 117) is printed inverted/garbled though its worked examples are correct, and the contents duplicates “Función Cuadrática” for Capítulo 3 Temas 1 and 4 and mislabels Capítulo 4 as “Capítulo III”; CONED 10: the falling-body model (p. 373) prints “12” for ½ (the worked values use ½ correctly), the key (p. 42) duplicates “tangentes” where “secantes” is meant, p. 14 asks for the area of a “circunferencia” (the circle), and the pentagon solution (p. 86) drops its degree symbols; CONED 11: the logarithm table (p. 118) misaligns its examples (it prints log₅ 5 = 2, which belongs to log₅ 25), the wound-model solution (p. 172) shows an unsigned division display before the correct result, and “invectiva” (p. 121) is a typo for inyectiva — are carried corrected on the rows they touch. One further note is carried as the placements carry it: the Medidas G3 answer key's building-height estimate reads 45 dam in the October copy and “4 dam” in the November extraction — treated as a re-export/extraction artefact in the key, not teaching content, and cited nowhere. Costa Rica's books are pupil books, so Costa Rica is COUNTED: it joins this lesson's acceptance count wherever the placements stock an item. CONED printed page = PDF page throughout; EVEPRIM pages are cited by PDF page with the book's section numbers (two exceptions print folios: the EVEPRIM 6 Probabilidad texto's printed folio = PDF page − 6 and the EVEPRIM 3 Medidas texto's = PDF page − 7; citations give PDF pages). Into this lesson the placements stock 17 items (items 46, 47, 70, 91, 92, 131, 132, 163, 164, 165, 179, 180, 181, 182, 189, 190 and 191).

## Cuba added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Cuba's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **29 in-hand entries = 25 distinct works (MINED, Editorial Pueblo y Educación, Cuba), every one read in full**: student textbooks for Grades 1 and 3–12, student workbooks for Grades 1, 2 and 3, and teacher documents (Programa and Orientaciones metodológicas) for Grades 1–5, 8 and 11 — and placed 247 teaching items (202 student-side, 45 teacher-evidence). **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 14 student + 8 teacher is WRONG** — the staging books.json holds 13 student + 9 teacher entries and textbooks.tsv holds 7 further secured entries; duplicated filings are cited once (the Grade 3 and Grade 8 student books were each verified in a second edition; the Grade 4 and Grade 5 student books are each filed twice as byte-identical copies — Grade 4 MD5 01c106741b186cf28d360854a40d7e93, Grade 5 MD5 b48e28b1df820e2451bb44eb33b5ac11). Record corrections the read proved, carried exactly: the record's “Matemática 2do grado Parte I student textbook” is in fact the COMPLETE Grade 2 workbook (Cuaderno de trabajo — Matemática 2, ISBN 978-959-13-1854-1) — **no Grade 2 student textbook is in hand**, and Grade 2 rests on its workbook, Programa and Orientaciones; the record's Grade 5 student byte size (36,868,096 B) is a truncated copy — the complete file read is 77,146,019 B / 396 pp; the Grade 11 teacher documents belong to the newer Perfeccionamiento programme and do **not** match the in-hand Grade 11 student book (2005 edition — its limits, derivatives and integrals chapters have no teacher companion in hand, and the programme's trigonometry unit has no chapter in that book), so they are filed as that programme's own teacher evidence, never as evidence for the 2005 book; the MINED portal source URLs now return soft-404 pages — the reads used Wayback captures of those same URLs and the GitHub routes. The placements' named exclusions — exercise sets, chapter exercises, self-evaluation sections, answers appendices, mathematical tables, historical feature essays and front/back matter — are stocked nowhere. Teacher items are TEACHER EVIDENCE — they state what the programme requires and how it is taught — are labelled as such on every row, and are never counted in this lesson's acceptance figure; student textbook and workbook items are COUNTED. Printed pages are the books' own printed folios as the placements cite them (each book's printed-page basis is stated in its book entry). Into this lesson the placements stock 1 item (items 239): 1 student item and 0 teacher-evidence items.

## Finland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Finland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **24 files / 23 distinct works of government-hosted open material (OPH and the aoe.fi Library of Open Educational Resources); Finland has NO national textbook series** — and placed 173 items (51 pupil + 122 teacher evidence). The records' kind labels are wrong for most of the set: 13 of the 18 Mathematics-labelled “student” files are actually teacher-facing (in-service course books, theory texts, activity series and instructor guides), and only 6 files are pupil-facing (the Metsämatikkaa cards, the quadrilateral chart, the weather game, the Päivänselvää chapter, the programming workbook and its model solutions) — every book below is carried at its TRUE kind, never the record label. One byte-identical duplicate (aoe.2674-alku = aoe.2686-alku, the same file under two aoe records) is cited once; the Päivänselvää book is a Chapter-2-only excerpt, cited only for what it holds; the quadrilateral chart's filed copy is 1 content page + 175 blank padding pages. The placements' named exclusions — the weather game's non-temperature content, the computing-pedagogy matter in the two Turku course books, the motivation and mindset material in the JoMa books, the Pikkumatikoita parts 2, 8 and 11, and the programming books' pure-programming tasks — are stocked nowhere. Pupil items are COUNTED normally; teacher-evidence items are named in the rows and stay outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 pupil items from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Perusmatematiikkaa ohjelmoimalla* (pupil workbook, aoe.1371) — Finland, Järvenpää, 2021, ISBN 978-952-94-4547-9; 3,056,839 bytes, 62 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page. PUPIL material — counted. The three year-books (Grades 7, 8, 9) in one PDF (Grade 7 printed pp. 6–22, Grade 8 pp. 24–40, Grade 9 pp. 42–62). Its model solutions (*Perusmatematiikkaa ohjelmoimalla — Malliratkaisut*, aoe.1371, 31 pp.) mirror it task by task, confirm every placement below, and are cited wherever this book is cited. Pure-programming tasks (strings, lists, passwords, game mechanics) are stocked nowhere — only tasks whose own content is mathematics.

Checked and NOT an error: *Matemática 4° Secundaria*, printed p. 84, prints Pythagoras as a² = b² + c² with a defined as the hypotenuse — correct as printed. The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Ireland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **9 pupil textbooks of the Text & Tests series (The Celtic Press / CJ Fallon): one English volume, Text & Tests 4 (New Edition), and eight Irish-language (Téacs & Trialacha) volumes** — and placed 570 items. Ireland has no government-published textbooks: every book in the set is a commercial in-use title held as a complete free copy. The English TT4 was read in phase3-staging (byte- and SHA-256-verified in place); the eight Irish volumes were re-grabbed from their recorded COGG (cogg.ie) source URLs, each landing byte-exact at its recorded size, every file's identity proved from its cover, copyright and contents pages. The expansion map's count is right: **9 student + 0 teacher** — the one further Ireland file in hand, *Dúchas 2 — Leabhar Scileanna*, is a Junior Cycle Irish-language (Gaeilge) skills book, not Maths, and is cited nowhere in this course. Extent, honestly: five whole-course books (TT1, TT2 GL, TT3, English TT4, TT6); three strand volumes complete as published but covering one strand only (TT4 Irish = Strand 2; TT5 Irish = Strand 1; TT7 Irish = Strand 5); and one supplement booklet (the TT5 Forlíonadh — deferred Strand 1 material and the Central Limit Theorem), complete as published, not a standalone textbook. Two record flags ride with the set (the read's flagged claims F1–F2, both about the library records, not the course): **F1** — 8 of the 9 PDFs are absent from their phase3-staging folders, which hold only books.json + CURRICULUM.md; all eight are secured in GitHub and were re-grabbed byte-exact from COGG for the read, and restoring them into staging is owed on the library side. **F2** — the held TT3 is the updated edition (2024) of the 2013 book, with an added Chapter 19, Inferential Statistics, that no record mentions; its three items are stocked from that chapter and TT3's book entry names the edition wherever it appears. The older strand books and the English TT4 New Edition teach much of the same Leaving Certificate Higher Level ground in two languages and editions (e.g. TT6 chs. 1–2 ≈ TT4 Eng chs. 1–2; TT7 ch. 2 ≈ TT4 Eng ch. 7): each book's row is stocked as its own citation — they are distinct held books, not duplicates — while Ireland itself is counted once in this lesson's taught-in figure. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of each book. The placements' named exclusions — every book's chapter tests (“Cuir triail ort féin”), tasks, learning-experience and review sections, chapter summaries and Answers sections, and every book's prefaces, keyword lists and contents pages — are stocked nowhere. Ireland COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Ireland book is a pupil textbook. The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Fiji's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 pupil textbooks of the Ministry of Education / Curriculum Development Unit series, one for each of Years 1–13** — and placed 1,012 items. The expansion map's count is exact: staging `books.json` holds exactly 13 Mathematics entries (one per year, all `kind: student`) and `phase3-push-state.json` lists all 13 as pushed; no Maths teacher guide is in hand (the AQEP Maths Guides for Lower Primary exist only as AnyFlip reader copies in the download list — not in hand, not counted). The staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, so each book was re-fetched for the read — from the repo tree (Contents API) where the pusher had stored it as a blob, else from the book's recorded `source_url` (a Wayback Machine capture of the ministry file, Beacon Media, or Google Drive) — and the grabbed copies were deleted after their placements were written. The read's nine flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) `textbooks.tsv` carries only 9 of the 13 rows (Years 1, 2, 3, 4, 7, 10, 11, 12, 13) — Years 5, 6, 8 and 9 are staged and pushed but missing from the TSV; (2) the Year 2 scan duplicates printed p. 57 and printed p. 58 is absent, so the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand to read and is cited nowhere; (3) the Year 5 capture has no cover/title leaves — the file opens at the Strand 1 divider and the body runs complete, Strand 1 p. 1 to Strand 5 p. 98, its identity resting on the staged record and the body's own structure; (4) Year 4's folio sequence is irregular — folios 44–48 and 54 never appear and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice — but the leaf count closes exactly (85 printed numbers − 6 absent + 2 duplicated = 81 body leaves), every contents strand and unit is present and no activity breaks mid-stream at the gaps, so the evidence points to the printed book's own folio misprints rather than missing leaves, a judgement the file alone cannot prove; (5) Year 3's scan order is scrambled at the end (contents folio ii and printed pp. 46, 51, 52 sit at PDF pp. 76–80) and one activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51 — counted once), with folio tracking accounting for all of printed pp. 1–75; (6) Year 10 exists in two packagings — the staged Beacon Media file (9,099,595 bytes, 202 pp.) is the copy cited; an ECopies capture of the same book is 9,079,205 bytes / 200 pp.; (7) Year 12's first Wayback transfer arrived truncated (11,794,148 bytes, broken cross-reference table) and was discarded — a fresh download delivered the complete book (17,085,310 bytes, 284 pp.), which is what was read; (8) Year 1's contents lists Chance at p. 65 and Data at p. 66 where the folios are 64 and 65 — the body count closes exactly, so nothing is missing and the contents is one out; (9) Year 6's interior copyright and acknowledgement pages misname it the “Year 7” book — a copy-paste error in the book itself; its cover metadata, contents, running heads and body are Year 6 throughout, and it is read and placed as Year 6. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach — among them Year 3's conversion-table lines “1,000 cm = 1 km” (correct: 100,000 cm) and “10,000 mm = 1,000 m” (correct: 10 m), Year 4's banner “30 minutes = 1 hour” against its own body's 60, Year 5's “1 000 millimetres = 1 litre”, Year 9's contents “Common Multitudes” (for Common Multiples) and its (a × b)ᵐ = aᵐ × bⁿ misprint, and Year 1's count-in-twos strip printing 9 where 19 belongs; Year 10 carries its own printed errata list (pp. 201–202), whose corrections are noted in the items. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching point — a named concept, method, rule or worked skill with its printed page. The placements' named exclusions — strand divider pages; contents and acknowledgement pages; pure exercise sets and worked-example repeats of an item already placed; Year 1's “Checking my number” ten-frame reference page (printed p. 32); Year 3's duplicated capacity page (counted once); Year 4's closing multiplication-table chart (printed p. 86); Year 10's printed errata list (pp. 201–202, apparatus — its corrections ride in the items); Year 12's closing motivational quotes and exam tips (printed pp. 273–275); Year 13's bibliography and back matter — are stocked nowhere. Fiji COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Fiji book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 31 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Mathematics Year 1*), Book 2 (*Mathematics Year 2*), Book 3 (*Mathematics Year 3*), Book 4 (*Mathematics Year 4*), Book 5 (*Year 5 Mathematics*), Book 6 (*Year 6 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 7 (*Mathematics Textbook Year 10*), Book 8 (*Mathematics for Year 11*), Book 9 (*Year 12 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 10 (*Year 13 Mathematics Textbook*).

## Honduras added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Honduras's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **21 files = 18 distinct works: 9 pupil textbooks (Grades 3–9, Grade 10 Matemática I and Grade 11 Matemática III BCH) and 12 teacher-guide files for Grades 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 11, the Grade 8 guide held in two editions (2018 and the 2022 revision) and the Grade 1 and 2 guides each in two scans** — all from the Secretaría de Educación's PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support — and placed 335 items (218 pupil-textbook items and 117 teacher-evidence items), plus 3 duplicate-edition records (placements items 241, 242 and 338) that are cited once with their works and carry no separate items. The expansion map's “9 student + 12 teacher” count is right: the staging `books.json` files, the `phase3-push-state.json` pushed set and `textbooks.tsv` agree on exactly these 21 files. There is no Grade 1–2 pupil textbook in the Secretaría's programme (workbook only — SE Manual de Lineamientos), so the Grade 1–2 teaching in hand is the two guides; there is no Grade 3 or Grade 6 teacher guide in hand. Every file was grabbed from its recorded `source_url` (educatrachos.hn / libroshonduras.com) after the staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, and verified by exact byte size against the download-list record and by page count against the `books.json` record before reading; grabbed copies were deleted after each book's text was extracted. Read basis, stated honestly: the six text-layer pupil books and eight text-layer guides were read in full from their text; the three scanned pupil books (Grades 7–9) were read by OCR of their Índices and unit/lesson openers with interior pages OCR-sampled — Grades 7 and 8 cross-checked against the full text-layer read of their own teacher guides, which plan every lesson class by class — and the two 2022 guides are themselves scans, OCR-read at cover, presentation and index level, the Grade 9 guide therefore placed at unit grain. Counting grain: one item per Índice lesson; a lesson teaching two distinct course topics is split into two items, and three items (a Grade 4 pupil lesson, a Grade 10 pupil lesson and a Grade 2 guide unit) each carry a row in two lessons below and are counted once. The placements' named exclusions — the “Páginas para recortar” cut-out pages of the Grades 3, 4 and 5 pupil books (manipulatives, not teaching text), the Grade 6 book's closing “Repaso” review, and the “Nos divertimos” / “¿Sabías que?” enrichment boxes inside placed lessons — are stocked nowhere. The read's flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) the download list §A's original 2026-10-21 mirror labels for the III-Ciclo files were swapped between Español and Matemáticas — the files it calls “Matemáticas 7/8/9 (2018 PROMETAM)” are the Español books, and its “Español 7/8/9” alternates are the Matemáticas books — a library-record fault, already corrected in `books.json`, reported here and not fixed by this merge; (2) the Grade 9 guide's cover misprints its cycle as “II Ciclo” where the programme is III Ciclo; (3) the Grade 11 pupil book's Índice misprints “Cincunferencias” for “Circunferencias”, taught corrected; (4) the 2014 scan of the Grade 1 guide has a materially poorer text layer than the mirror scan of the same work, so anyone text-mining it alone will under-read it. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items — 2 pupil-textbook rows and 2 teacher-evidence rows — from Book 6 (*Matemática III (BCH), Libro del Estudiante, Undécimo grado*), Book 12 (*Matemática III (BCH), Guía del Docente*), Book 14 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 8vo grado (2018)*), Book 16 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas and 8vo grado*).

**Book 6:** *Matemática III (BCH), Libro del Estudiante, Undécimo grado* — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación; PROMETAM Fase III, UPNFM/JICA, 1ª ed. 2017, rev. 2018; 8,739,380 bytes, 98 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page − 8. Pupil textbook. Book error carried: its Índice and the Unidad II Lección 2 opener spell “Cincunferencias” for “Circunferencias” — a misprint in the book, taught corrected.

**Book 12:** *Matemática III (BCH), Guía del Docente* (teacher guide) — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación; 7,719,993 bytes, 134 PDF pp. Teacher evidence only: class plans for Book 6's lessons — its rows cite the pupil book's printed pages the plan supports.

## Italy added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Italy's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **14 pupil textbooks, all adopted commercial titles (Italy has no government textbooks; schools adopt titles under the AIE/MIUR adozioni system): the Bergamini *Matematica.blu* volumes (Zanichelli) and the Sasso *Colori della Matematica* Edizione Verde and Edizione Blu volumes and triennio modules A–G (Petrini / De Agostini Scuola)** — and placed 781 items. The expansion map's “14 student + 0 teacher” count and kind split are exact against the staging `books.json` files, `textbooks.tsv` and the push state; every file was verified against its record (byte size, and MD5 where the record carries one) and read in full. Counting grain: one item per numbered theory section of the book's own Indice/Sommario. Two extent faults ride with the set, honestly: the files filed as *Matematica.blu* Vol. 1 (Book 3) and *Matematica.blu 2.0* Vol. 5 (Book 4) are each only the SECOND PART of the volume — Vol. 1 opens at Capitolo 13 (printed p. 670) and its Capitoli 1–12 and G1–G4 are not in the file; Vol. 5 opens at Capitolo 25 (printed p. 1560) and its Capitoli 18–24 are not in the file — so both are named as partial volumes wherever they are cited, only their held content is placed, and the missing first parts are not in hand anywhere and are cited nowhere (the ground the Vol. 1 first part would have covered is supplied inside this set by Books 5–6 at Sasso depth, and the Vol. 5 first part's limits and continuity ground by Book 12, Modulo G). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the eBook-only units the books' own Indici mark “(solo nell'eBook)”; and the apparatus around the numbered theory sections in every book — chapter openings, exercise blocks, laboratories, *Realtà e modelli*, exam-preparation sections (*Verso l'esame*, *Verso l'INVALSI*, *Verso l'università*), glossaries, answer keys, formula sheets, and *Approfondimento* boxes that are not numbered theory sections. Into this lesson the placements stock 27 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Matematica.blu — Vol. 2*), Book 2 (*Matematica.blu 2.0 — Vol. 4*), Book 3 (*Matematica.blu — Vol. 1*, second part only), Book 7 (*Colori della Matematica — Edizione Blu, Vol. 2*) and Book 9 (*Colori della Matematica — Edizione Blu, Modulo E*).

**Book 1:** Bergamini/Trifone/Barozzi, *Matematica.blu — Vol. 2: Algebra, Geometria, Probabilità* (Zanichelli, 2010; filed at Grade 10) — Italy; 12,160,155 bytes, 907 PDF pp., MD5 identical to the source record's. Complete as published: the volume opens at Capitolo 9 (printed p. 633), its pagination continuous from Vol. 1. Pupil textbook (adopted commercial title).

**Book 2:** Bergamini/Trifone/Barozzi, *Matematica.blu 2.0 — Vol. 4* (Zanichelli, 2011; filed at Grade 12) — Italy; 11,710,476 bytes, 740 PDF pp., MD5 identical to the source record's. ## Mauritius added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The expansion map is WRONG in substance for Mauritius (flag F2): it recorded “1 student / 0 teacher” in hand, but the counted file is a supplementary worksheets excerpt, not a pupil textbook, and the Progress Book was not counted at all; the true in-hand Maths-content set is 0 pupil textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 1 pupil worksheets excerpt and 1 progress-record book. ## Mozambique added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Mozambique’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **15 files: 14 pupil Matemática modules of the ministry’s PESD distance-learning programme (PESD I Módulos 1–5, studied by the 1º ciclo = grades 7–9; PESD II Módulos 1–9, studied by the 2º ciclo = grades 10–12) and 1 teacher guide, the *Manual do Professor — 1.ª Classe*, whose Matemática part (printed pp. 146–183) is teacher evidence** — all from the Ministério da Educação e Desenvolvimento Humano (MINEDH) through its Instituto de Educação Aberta e à Distância (IEDA) — and placed 233 items (227 pupil-textbook items and 6 teacher-evidence items). Mozambique’s basis, by the user’s 2026-11-02 ruling, is this ministry distance-learning (PESD) set, not the commercial print set: the modules are studied by cycle, not per grade, and are cited as distance-learning books wherever they appear. The expansion map’s “14 student” count is exact, but its “0 teacher” is wrong — the teacher guide is filed under subject “general” at grade-01 and carries a full Matemática part; the true in-hand set is 14 pupil + 1 teacher guide. The three staging `books.json` files, `textbooks.tsv` and `phase3-push-state.json` agree on exactly these 15 files; no staging PDF remained on local disk (pusher-secured, local copies deleted), so every file was fetched fresh from its recorded official source, ieda.gov.mz, and matched its recorded byte size exactly; all 15 MD5s are distinct — no duplicates — and the generically named PESD II file `Modulo-9.pdf` is proved by its title page to be Matemática Módulo 9. Printed-page basis: PESD I modules — printed page = PDF page − 1 (footer-verified); PESD II modules — printed page = the contents-page foliation; the *Manual do Professor* — printed page = PDF page. Counting grain: one item per lesson of a module where the lesson teaches one course topic; a lesson is split into two items where it teaches two clearly distinct course topics. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected, as the placements record them: PESD II Módulo 3’s cover subtitle wrongly repeats Módulo 1’s (“Conjuntos Numéricos e Cálculo Algébrico”) — its interior is exponential and logarithmic functions throughout — and its answer section is headed “Soluções Modulo 1” (sic) although it is Módulo 3’s own key; PESD II Módulo 9’s answer section is headed “Soluções Módulo 8” (sic) although it is Módulo 9’s own key; PESD II Módulo 5’s contents numbers two different lessons “Lição 4” (the linear-function revision, printed p. 43, and the modulus function, printed p. 55) — both lessons are placed, as items 177 and 178 of this read; and PESD I Módulo 4’s contents page omits its Lição 13, which exists in the body at printed p. 64 and is placed (item 96), while its Lições 14 and 15 carry identical stated objectives and are placed as two items as printed (items 97 and 98). The placements’ record items are apparatus, stocked nowhere: R1 — every PESD module’s closing preparation test (teste de preparação), answer key (chave de correcção / soluções) and bibliography; R2 — the *Manual do Professor*’s answer key to the 1.ª Classe pupil book (printed pp. 174–183; the pupil book itself is not in hand); R3 — the DGD Módulo 1 spot-check copy, fetched only to verify its subject identity. The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: DGD (Desenho e Geometria Descritiva, PESD II Módulos 1, 3–12 — a separate named subject of the Mozambican curriculum with its own module series, interior-checked on Módulo 1 and excluded as such; Módulo 2 is not in hand at all); all other PESD subjects in hand (Biologia, Química, Física, Geografia, História, Inglês, Português, Francês, TIC, Agro-pecuária, Empreendedorismo, Educação Visual, Filosofia) — separate curriculum subjects, none a Matemática book in disguise; the *Manual do Professor*’s Língua Portuguesa and Educação Física parts (printed pp. 4–145 and 184–215); and the primary print-programme Matemática books (1ª–6ª, Edição revista 2025), which are link-only, not in hand and not read. Pupil items are COUNTED normally; the teacher-evidence items (T1–T6, from the one teacher guide) are named in the rows and stay outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 items — 3 pupil-textbook rows — from Book 14 (*Matemática — Módulo 9 (PESD II)*).

Pupil textbook — a PESD distance-learning module, studied by cycle (2º ciclo = grades 10–12), not per grade. Its answer section is headed “Soluções Módulo 8” (sic) — it is Módulo 9’s own key.

## Nauru added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Nauru's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **10 documents, all of them Learning Through Doing (LTD) *Mathematics Planning Documents*, Nauru editions (2026) — Prep and Years 1–8, plus the LTD *Scope and Sequence, Years 1–8* — teacher planning alignments that sequence an Australian commercial subscription lesson system against the Nauru Mathematics Syllabus, every lesson keyed to a Nauru curriculum code (NRU-*); 0 pupil (student) textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 0 workbooks** — and placed 238 items, every one of them PLANNING/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE: the Nauru syllabus's own key-idea statements as printed on each document's closing “Nauru Curriculum Alignment” pages. Nauru publishes no government textbooks (country record, download list §§F–Q, seven fronts), so Nauru's standing in the course is the syllabus-evidence reckoning: Nauru is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count is right in number and wrong in kind: it carries Nauru as “10 student Maths books”, but every one of the 10 books.json entries is kind `planning-document` and states in its own approval note that it is NOT a Nauru government student textbook (diff fault F1 — a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge). textbooks.tsv carries 12 Nauru rows, all of them USP IOE ESD items, and zero Mathematics rows — the 10 planning documents live only in books.json and push-state. Australia overlap is ZERO: the Australia read's nine Cambridge/Jacaranda Years 8–10 pupil textbooks share no title, ISBN, byte size or file with this set, and the QCE senior titles cross-referenced in Nauru's country record are link-only, in hand for neither country and read by neither reader; nothing from the Australia placements is cited here. All 10 files were read from local disk in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records (334,327–887,725 bytes; 67 PDF pages in total; 10 distinct MD5s, no duplicates inside the set); nothing was downloaded. Printed-page basis: the documents carry no printed folios (the Scope's footer “Page N” equals its PDF page), so pages cited are document (PDF) pages — Prep key ideas on alignment pp. 6–7 of 7, Years 1–8 on alignment p. 6 of 6; the term overviews (p. 1) and weekly plans (pp. 2–5) sequence the same key ideas and add none of their own. Counting grain: one item per NRU key idea. The *Scope and Sequence* (887,725 bytes, 13 PDF pages) was read in full and cross-checked — every Scope topic traces to a year document's lessons under the codes placed — so it contributes 0 items of its own; counting it again would double-count one programme as two sources. Book errors found on the read are placed corrected, as the placements record them (diff fault F2): Year 3 NRU-M3.3 prints “suing” for “using” and NRU-G3.3 prints “jey” for “key”; Year 5 NRU-CD5.4 prints “recoding sheets” for “recording sheets”; Year 8 NRU-PA8.4 prints “co-ordiinates”; the Year 8 alignment page prints its first geometry code as “NRU-G9.1” — a Year 9 code inside the Year 8 document — while the Year 8 weekly plans cite the same lessons as NRU-G8.1, and item 226 is placed as the Year 8 item it is; the Year 2 document's Term 4 plan cites “NRU-N3.4” against Year 2 strategy-review lessons, where the alignment page's NRU-N2.4 governs; several weekly-plan code strings are garbled in print — the alignment pages' code lists are clean and govern throughout. The placements also record a document inconsistency (diff fault F3): the Scope's fractions table opens its halves entry at the earliest (Prep) level, but the Prep planning document contains no fractions anywhere — fractions enter the year documents at Year 1 (NRU-N1.8) — and the placements follow the year documents. Some key ideas are explicitly Nauru-localised (Year 3 NRU-G3.3: simple maps of Nauru; Year 6 NRU-CD6.4: data contexts “familiar to Nauruan students”; Year 8 NRU-PA8.6: graphs “using data from Nauru”). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the Nauru Mathematics Syllabus aims paragraph printed in every year document (general aims, no subject content); the term overviews and weekly plans as separate items (they sequence the same key ideas, and the LTD lesson titles are the Australian system's commercial catalogue, delivered by subscription — the planning documents do not reproduce the lessons, so no lesson content is placed, only the syllabus key ideas the lessons are sequenced against); “Assessment Opportunities” headers and blank assessment columns (apparatus); the Scope & Sequence's duplicated topic statements across years (one programme, counted once at key-idea grain); the 12 USP IOE ESD files filed under Nauru (not Mathematics, excluded by subject); and the OER4Pacific COL open-schooling Mathematics 11/12 PDFs named in the download list §G (regional OER, never staged under Nauru, not in hand, not read). Into this lesson the placements stock 14 planning/curriculum-evidence items from Book 1 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 1, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 2 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 3, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 3 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 4, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 4 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 5, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 5 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 6, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 6 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 7, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 7 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 8, Nauru edition (2026)*).

## Nigeria added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Nigeria’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **11 files: 1 pupil textbook and 10 teacher guides** — and placed 199 items (20 pupil-teaching items from the pupil textbook and 179 teacher-evidence items from the ten guides). The pupil textbook is *New Concept Mathematics for Senior Secondary Schools 1* (3rd ed. 2016, Learn Africa, 352 pp., 19,071,603 B), held as a release asset in `mid-size-books-8`; the guides are 7 Learn Africa guides (New Method Primary 1; New Concept JSS 1–3 and SS 1–3) and 3 Cambridge *Excellence in Mathematics* teacher’s guides (Books 1–3). The expansion map’s kind split (0 student + 10 teacher + 1 link-only) is wrong on the student count — the SS1 pupil textbook is in hand, and the map’s “1 link-only” is plausibly this same book misclassified; the true split is 1 student + 10 teacher, 11 files (flag F1, record-side). Record discrepancy (flag F2, record-side): the records describe the held JSS 1 teacher’s guide as a 2024 file of 396,871 B (4th edition), but the file in hand and read is the **2026 fifth edition** (362,534 B, ISBN 978 978 925 9755); the 2024 file is not in hand. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected (flag F3): the JSS 1 guide’s Ch20 states “1 hour = 1°” on the clock face — 1 hour = 30°; the JSS 2 guide prints “99 300 = 9.03 × 10⁴” (should be 9.93 × 10⁴), works a simple-interest example stated with T = 4 years at 3 years, and gives the Pythagorean triple “(5, 12, 23)” (should be (5, 12, 13)); the SS 1 guide prints the sector-area formula as θ/360 × 2πr² (should be θ/360 × πr²) and describes standard-form A as “between 0 and 9” (should be 1 ≤ A < 10); the SS 2 guide works log 0.000 592 and prints the characteristic without its negative/bar sign, and conflates “complementary” with “independent” events in places; the SS 3 guide calls A⁻¹ “the negative matrix” in one section (it is the inverse matrix, used correctly later), with several standard-integral lines and one index law garbled in print. The Primary 1 guide’s printed Contents page numbers do not match its body folios (flag F4) — its items cite body folios throughout. Printed-page basis: Learn Africa guide items cite the guide’s own printed (folio) pages — chapter starts from each book’s contents, verified against body folios; pupil-book items cite the pupil book’s printed pages; Cambridge items cite the guide’s printed topic-start page, each topic’s Student’s Book page span being given in the book’s scheme of work. Counting grain: one item per teaching chapter (Learn Africa books) or per teaching topic (Cambridge guides). The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: revision and exam-practice apparatus across the series (the SS1 pupil book’s Preparatory chapter and its three Revision-exercise parts; Cambridge TG1 Topics 1 and 16, TG2 Topics 1 and 18, TG3 Topics 1 and 13 and TG3’s sample examination papers); the SS 3 guide’s Ch16 “Numbers and numeration”, an explicit revision chapter; reference apparatus (the pupil book’s Tables and formulae, Four-figure tables, Answers and Index; the Cambridge guides’ Introduction sections); and the non-Maths Nigeria files checked and excluded (the English, Economics, Government, Commerce, Biology, Chemistry, Physics and Literature books in hand, the FME approved-textbooks list, and the 6 TVET trades curriculum documents — none is a Maths book). Pupil items are COUNTED normally; teacher-guide items are worded as teacher evidence and stay outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 items — all teacher-evidence rows — from Books 3, 8 and 9.

**Book 3:** *New Concept Mathematics for JSS 2 — Teacher's Guide*, 4th edition 2023 — Nigeria, Learn Africa, ISBN 978 978 925 8000; 610,038 bytes, 96 PDF pp. Teacher guide — teacher evidence only.

**Book 8:** *New Concept Mathematics for SS 2 — Teacher's Guide*, 4th edition 2024 — Nigeria, Learn Africa, ISBN 978 978 925 826 0; 439,955 bytes, 44 PDF pp. Teacher guide — teacher evidence only.

**Book 9:** *Excellence in Mathematics — Teacher's Guide Book 2* — Nigeria, Cambridge University Press, 2016, ISBN 978-1-316-60876-0; 3,565,576 bytes, 190 PDF pp. Teacher guide — teacher evidence only. Items cite the guide's printed topic-start page; each topic's Student's Book span (pp. 1–307) is given in the book's scheme of work.

|---|---|
| Probability: chance, sample space, events and the probability of an event | Nigeria — *New Concept Mathematics JSS 2 TG (Learn Africa)* (teacher guide — teacher evidence), TG printed p. 89 |
| Probability (experimental and theoretical; mutually exclusive, independent and complementary events; addition and multiplication laws; with and without replacement; Venn and tree diagrams) | Nigeria — *New Concept Mathematics SS 2 TG (Learn Africa)* (teacher guide — teacher evidence), TG printed p. 37 |
| Probability (equiprobable sample spaces, experimental probability, complementary, addition and multiplication rules, with and without replacement) | Nigeria — *Excellence in Mathematics TG Book 2 (Cambridge)* (teacher guide — teacher evidence), TG printed p. 159 |

## Peru added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Peru's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **3 pupil WORKBOOKS of the Ministerio de Educación (MINEDU): *Cuaderno de trabajo Matemática* for Grades 4, 1 and 2** — and placed 238 items, one per *ficha* (the workbook's own numbered teaching unit). The expansion map's count is right and its kind label is wrong (record correction C1): the 3 are **workbooks**, not student textbooks — no Peru pupil *texto*, teacher guide or secondary Maths book is in hand, and every other Peru Maths title in the records (Primaria Matemática 3, 5, 6; Secundaria *Resolvamos problemas* 1–5; *Fichas de Matemática* 1–5; the multigrado/rural *textos*) is link-only, never downloaded. The fourth secured Peru file, the Grade 1 *Ciencia y Tecnología* fichas booklet (7 PDF pages, 18,486,216 bytes), was checked first-hand and is science only — no maths content — and is cited nowhere here. A workbook is a pupil book: every item below is **teaching content** (each ficha teaches its topic through worked situations, definitions in context and guided practice), so Peru's rows are pupil rows, COUNTED in full — the workbook set closes as a teaching set, not an evidence-only one (the South Africa pattern). The secured files are **complete volumes** (each runs from its cover to the standard MINEDU civics back matter at its printed end) and are not duplicates of one another (distinct byte sizes and MD5s). They are, though, **later printings than the records describe** (record correction C2): all three are the 2021 Dotación-2022 printings — Grade 4 the 5th edition of June 2021 (Depósito Legal BNP N.° 2021-08052), not the 2016 first edition of its download-list mirror note; Grade 1 likewise the 5th edition of June 2021 (Depósito Legal BNP N.° 2021-08267), not the 3rd edition of 2019 (Dotación 2020) its mirror note describes — and Grade 2's credits page carries both the 2019 printing (C. P. N.° 002-2019-MINEDU/VMGP/UE 120, Dotación 2020) and the 5th edition of June 2021 (Depósito Legal BNP N.° 2021-08050) over one shared reedition body; the citations below carry the printings actually read. ## Jamaica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Jamaica's whole in-hand Maths set from the files — **8 documents: 7 National Standards Curriculum (NSC) guides that carry a Mathematics section (Grades 1, 2 and 3 Integrated Studies / Language Arts / Mathematics; Grades 4, 5 and 6 Exploratory Core; and the Grades 7–9 Mathematics guide — all Ministry of Education, Youth and Information, © 2018, the Grade 6 guide in its FINAL edition of Jan 08 2020), plus 1 pupil reader** — and placed 311 items: 308 curriculum-evidence items from the guides and 3 pupil-text items from the reader. **Kind split: 0 pupil textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 7 curriculum guides.** A curriculum guide states what is REQUIRED to be taught and how to teach it; it is not a pupil textbook, so every guide item below is CURRICULUM EVIDENCE, worded “curriculum requires”, and Jamaica is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The expansion map is wrong on both count and kind (flag F1, record-side, reported not fixed): it listed Jamaica as FILES-IN-HAND with 4 student / 0 teacher Maths books — its build matched the subject field only, counting the three Grades 1–3 guides and the Grades 7–9 guide and missing the three Grades 4–6 Exploratory Core guides, whose subject field reads only “Exploratory Core” while their title pages name Mathematics as one of the four core subjects; and the four files it did count are curriculum guides, not student books. Two further record faults ride with the set (record-side; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/jamaica.md filing exists or was created): textbooks.tsv has **no row at all for the Grades 7–9 Mathematics guide** (flag F2), although the file is in staging, in books.json and in the pushed set; and the TSV's Grade 5 row points at a Telegram link instead of the file's secured GitHub asset (flag F3) — the book was read byte-exact from that asset. One file defect, stated plainly: the Grade 5 guide's body font has a broken ToUnicode map, so its text layer extracts as bullet glyphs; its Mathematics section was read in full from rendered page images (120-DPI renders of PDF pp. 139–255 = printed pp. 122–238), every page legible, and it is counted as read on that basis. Printed-page basis: each guide prints its own folio; the Mathematics windows run printed = PDF − 5 (Grade 1), − 33 (Grade 2), − 16 (Grade 3), − 11 (Grade 4), − 17 (Grade 5) and − 12 (Grade 6), and Book 7's pages are its own per-grade section pages. Counting grain: Grades 1–3, one item per distinct numbered objective in the Mathematics Content Standards, plus NEW/Extended objectives and Scope-and-Sequence topics stated in no standard (marked (S&S)); Grades 4–6 and 7–9, one item per distinct objective in each Mathematics unit's objectives list; an objective restated verbatim is counted once, at its first full statement. Boundary files examined and excluded — stocked nowhere: the nursery *Birth to Three is Key* early-childhood guide (ages 0–3, all-domains; number concepts are one activity focus, with no Mathematics section or objectives at NSC grain — not a Maths book); the Grades 4–6 Resource & Technology guide (its file is a bundle whose opening leaves are the Grades 7–9 English guide; no Mathematics section); and the Civics (×2), English Language, Integrated Science and Social Studies guides (no Mathematics subject in any of them; the Science guide's measurement/graphing is science content). The placements' named exclusions inside the books are likewise stocked nowhere: the Grade 1 window's “Maths Prayers” (printed pp. 242–243 — devotional poems, religious apparatus, no mathematics content); the Scope and Sequence charts, Process Strand checklists, Illustrative Activities, Benchmarks and Illustrative Examples as apparatus (topics stated only there are placed as items and marked (S&S)); the Integrated Studies and Language Arts windows of the Grades 1–3 guides, which restate the same Mathematics attainment targets as delivery vehicles and state no requirement beyond the window's standards; the Grade 1 book's Mathematics lesson plans and “Teaching Mathematics: Ideas & Activities” (printed pp. 340–400, teacher apparatus); Book 7's Appendices A–E and closing sample lesson plan (printed pp. 191–206, activity apparatus and worked examples of the same objectives); and the reader's copyright/credits leaves. Book errors and faults are carried as the placements record them, taught corrected where they touch a row: the Grade 1 fractions standard (printed p. 275) cross-refers an objective as “Objective 2.3.27” — a Grade 2 bank number inside the Grade 1 book — and objective numbers with a leading “2.” recur throughout the Grade 1 standards (the bank numbering is shared across grades; each such objective is placed once, as stated for Grade 1); the Grade 2 addition example on printed p. 268 (“Pat has 43 oranges … How many apples Pat has now?”) mixes fruits mid-problem — a wording slip, the objective it illustrates placed as read; two Grade 2 standards pages carry a mojibake line (a Caesar-shifted “See previous page for Attainment Targets and Objectives”) — a print-production artefact, no content lost; the Grade 3 window and its Scope and Sequence state **no decimals** anywhere, so no Grade 3 decimals row is stocked; the Grade 4 Term 3 Unit 2 outcome names “cross-sections” of cubes/cuboids, which the unit nowhere teaches — placed inside its item as named-only; the Grade 4 probability unit's header prints “GRAGE 4” (sic); the Grade 5 Overview disagrees with its own units three times, recorded as printed (division of five-digit numbers sits in Term 3 Unit 1, not Term 2; the Overview's Volume has no objective anywhere in the Grade 5 Mathematics section and is stocked nowhere; lines of symmetry sit in Term 2 Unit 3, while the Term 3 Geometry unit is circles, polygons to eight sides, and pyramids); the Grade 6 Term 3 Unit 1 tyre-pressure activity is Science-linked and its arithmetic is already inside its unit's item, not placed separately; in Book 7, the Grade 9 ratio unit's footer prints “NSC Mathematics: Grade 7” (a template error — the unit sits in the Grade 9 section and is placed as Grade 9), the matrices unit header prints “GRADE 9 TERM 1: NUMBERS” although the unit sits in the Term 3 sequence (placed as Term 3), the Grade 7 constructions unit's objectives page repeats the transformations unit's first objective (counted once, in the transformations item), and the Grade 8 Term 2 geometry unit carries a header title copied from the algebra unit while its objectives are polygons and angle sums (placed by its objectives). Jamaica's pupil Maths textbooks are commercial (Carlong / Hodder / Macmillan etc.) — the library holds none; they stand in the download list only, not in hand and not read. Pages cited are the book's own per-grade section pages (“NSC Mathematics: Grade 7/8/9, p. N”). CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY. textbooks.tsv carries no row for this guide at all (flag F2, record-side, reported not fixed).

## Haiti added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Haiti's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **28 files, every one of them teacher-side**: the nine *Programme à Compétences Minimales* (PCM) volumes for Années Fondamentales 1–9 (MENFP, Direction de l'Enseignement Fondamental, November 2019), the three IPN *Programme détaillé, Matières de base* volumes (4e, 5e, 6e année), the three *Programme Pédagogique Opérationnel* (PPO) Mathématiques fascicules for the 3e cycle (7e, 8e, 9e année, 1989-1990), the eight Nectar (MENFP) teacher sequences (four for 7e année, four for 8e année), the Nouveau Secondaire *Programme-Cadre* and the four NS detailed programmes (NS1–NS4) — every one of the 28 carrying a full Mathématiques section — and placed **1,639 items, every one of them TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE**: **Haiti has no pupil Maths textbook in hand under any label**, so Haiti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Ghana / Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count for Haiti is wrong: it carries 15, counting only the books.json entries whose subject field reads “Mathematics”, and the download list claims 43 teacher files; the true maths-bearing set is 28 files — the nine “All subjects” PCM volumes, the three IPN detailed programmes and the NS Programme-Cadre all carry full maths sections the count missed (a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge); textbooks.tsv is likewise a stale 28-row index naming only a subset of the secured files. Haiti's official pupil books (the MENFP/FAGEM *Manuels uniques* / “Mon manuel” series) are in hand for other subjects only, never for Mathematics, and the two remanié programme leads (a 7e année fascicule and a 4e année detailed programme) are not in hand — and programme documents are not textbooks under the 2026-11-10 textbook-test ruling in any case — so **Haiti stays PARTIAL for Maths, teacher evidence only**. All 28 files were read page-by-page from the secured PDFs in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records; nothing was downloaded. Read depth: the PPO 8e fascicule and the IPN 4e and 6e programmes are image-only scans and were read by OCR in full, spot-checked against the page images — where OCR garbled a worked example's digits, the placement teaches the corrected arithmetic and the case is flagged; PPO 7e PDF p. 48 and PPO 9e PDF pp. 20, 30, 31 and 32 were verified blank, separator or title pages carrying no objectives. Printed-page bases: PCM printed page = PDF page − 1; IPN 4e and 6e printed page = PDF page − 9, IPN 5e printed folios equal PDF pages; PPO 7e printed = PDF − 3, PPO 8e printed folio = PDF page, PPO 9e printed = PDF − 2; Programme-Cadre NS printed = PDF − 9 (verified against the detailed programme at §5.1–§5.2); NS1 and NS2 printed = PDF page, NS3 cites PDF pages, NS4 printed = PDF − 1; the Nectar sequences cite PDF pages by séance. The placements' eleven flags are all document-side and are carried here exactly: the PCM 6e volume's maths section header mislabels its level “5ème AF” (F1 — its items are cited to the 6e volume they stand in); PCM 5e's spoken-number row slips (“six mille deux” for 6,002, and a thousands-family slip in item 352's row) are placed corrected (F2); the PCM “orthocentre” definition error stands in the PCM **7e** volume — the diff's “(AF8)” label is the diff's own slip, disproven against the live PCM 8e section, which carries the correct circumcentre definition (F3); PCM 9e's right-asymptote and strict-inequality rows are completions of points the volume leaves silent, not errors (F4); PCM 9e's opening “années diagnostiques” ((6e), 7e, 8e) reinstate the diagnostic year the 3e-cycle fascicules dropped (F5); the PCM 7e volume announces a simple-interest objective that does not exist anywhere in its grids — nothing is placed for it, and simple interest is taught by PCM 9e and PPO 9e (F6); the Nectar “Relations entre les angles” sequence states a false corresponding-angles criterion and a mise en train with no solution, and the “Calcul littéral” sequence's séance 5 example drops a factor of x — all placed as corrected (F7, F8); the record-side faults (the map's count, the download list's count, the stale textbooks.tsv) are reported, not fixed (F9); the IPN 6e worked examples carry numerical inconsistencies (objectives 5.1, 7.1, 12.2, 16.1, 19.1, 21.1, 22.1) flagged as read and NOT asserted as print errors — the volume is an OCR-read scan and some may be digit misreads (F10); and NS2's own dating (2015 in the detailed programmes' shared headers, the school year 2008-2009 on its cover) is the book's own, not a discrepancy (F11). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the PCM volumes' other-discipline sections and their week-by-week scheduling columns beyond each distinct objective; the IPN volumes' maths avant-propos, theme indexes, other-subject sections and Suggestions/Évaluation apparatus (except where an activity is itself the taught content); the PPO fascicules' shared front matter, Programme-Cadre theme tables, progression grids, bibliographies and annexes; the Nectar sequences' summative-evaluation séances and session-management apparatus (except where a séance's activity is the taught content); the Programme-Cadre's other subjects, and the Nouveau Secondaire framework chapters shared between it and the NS1/NS2 volumes (placed once, from the Programme-Cadre volume); and the verified blank and separator pages. Into this lesson the placements stock 9 items — all teacher/curriculum-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*PCM, 9e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 2 (*PPO Mathématiques, 9e année (1989-1990)*), Book 3 (*Programme-Cadre NS (MENFP)*), Book 4 (*NS3 Programme détaillé (MENFP)*) and Book 5 (*NS4 Programme détaillé (MENFP)*).

**Book 1:** *Programme à Compétences Minimales (PCM), 9e Année Fondamentale* (curriculum programme) — Haiti, MENFP, Direction de l'Enseignement Fondamental (DEF), November 2019; 1,845,452 bytes, 78 PDF pages. TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the ministry programme's required content for the year, not a pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page − 1.

**Book 2:** *Programme Pédagogique Opérationnel, 3e cycle du Fondamental, Fascicule 4: Mathématiques, 9e année* (programme fascicule, 1989-1990) — Haiti, MENFP / Institut Pédagogique National; 5,173,324 bytes, 81 PDF pages. TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the operational programme's objectives for the year, not a pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page − 2. (PDF pp. 20, 30, 31 and 32 were verified blank, separator or title pages carrying no objectives.)

**Book 3:** *Programme-Cadre de toutes les Matières, Nouveau Secondaire* (secondary framework) — Haiti, MENFP; 1,626,261 bytes, 172 PDF pages. TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the Nouveau Secondaire framework's mathematics chapter (PDF pp. 120–134), not a pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page − 9 (verified against the detailed programme at §5.1–§5.2).

**Book 4:** *Programme détaillé de Mathématiques, Nouveau Secondaire 3 (NS3)* (detailed programme) — Haiti, MENFP; 262,493 bytes, 14 PDF pages (mathematics on PDF pp. 5–14). TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the year's detailed teaching programme, not a pupil textbook. Citations are PDF pages.

**Book 5:** *Programme détaillé de Mathématiques, Nouveau Secondaire 4 (NS4)* (detailed programme) — Haiti, MENFP; 2,975,332 bytes, 42 PDF pages. TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the year's detailed teaching programme, not a pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page − 1.
