## Papua New Guinea added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Papua New Guinea’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **32 files = 28 works, all Papua New Guinea Department of Education (NDoE), “Issued free to schools by the Department of Education”: 8 pupil textbook files (7 works — the JICA/QUIS-ME National Mathematics Textbooks, Grades 1–6, with Grades 3 and 5 in two parts each) and 24 teacher files (21 works — 9 JICA teacher’s manual files, 6 works with Grades 3–5 in two parts each, and 15 Standards Based Curriculum (SBC) teacher guides, Elementary Prep to Grade 12, with separate Advanced and General guides at Grades 11 and 12)** — and placed 503 items (102 from the pupil textbooks, 401 teacher-evidence items from the manuals and guides). The expansion map is wrong on both of its numbers (flag 1 — record-side, reported, not fixed by this merge): it said 6 student + 22 teacher in hand; the true set is 8 student + 24 teacher. The map missed three files that exist only as textbooks.tsv rows (the Grade 4 and Grade 6 full-year JICA pupil textbooks and the Grade 6 JICA manual) and did not know the Grade 2 SBC guide, which has a staging books.json row but no textbooks.tsv row and no push-state entry (flag 4). **Filing error (flag 2 — record-side): the git-tree copies recorded for the Grade 1 and Grade 2 JICA manuals are a different country’s book** — both TSV tree paths hold the identical file, a Mozambique Grade 1 teacher manual in Portuguese (“Manual do Professor, 1.ª classe”, 5,891,019 B / 216 pp, the same MD5 at both paths — the only byte-identical duplicate pair found in this hunt, and not a PNG book), which also sits in the release `mid-size-books-8` correctly named as a Mozambique book. The genuine PNG manuals were read from the GitHub release `full-quality-oversized-books`, whose assets match the staged sizes and checklist MD5s exactly; the push-state’s per-folder landing claims for those two files should be treated as unreliable until the tree paths are re-filed. Flag 3: the two manuals’ recorded education.gov.pg source URLs do not serve the staged pressings — both returned an HTML page during the read, and a later re-probe served a different pressing of the Grade 1 manual — so the staged pressings cannot be re-grabbed from the recorded source URLs. Flag 5: the Grade 9 SBC guide’s textbooks.tsv tree path 404s; the file was recovered by searching all release assets and matches books.json on size and pages. No Maths hides under other subject labels in the PNG records — all 156 staging books.json entries and all 163 textbooks.tsv rows were checked by subject, title and path. Link-only Maths not in hand and not read (it does not count against closure): the FODE distance course-books for Grades 7–12 Mathematics / Maths A / Maths G (unit PDFs) and the two OBE upper-secondary guides found 2026-11-10 (Advanced Mathematics TG, ISBN 978-9980-9924-8-2; General Mathematics TG). Book errors found on the read are taught corrected: the Grade 1 Textbook and its manual print “Subtracation” in the Chapter/Unit 12 title; the SBC guides print “Angels” for Angles in places, “Algebrain” for Algebraic (a Grade 11 guide’s Factorisation unit header) and “Orobability” for Probability (the Grade 9 guide’s strand table); the Grade 1 SBC guide’s Term-1 record prints “tenths” where tens is meant. The Grade 3 SBC programme has no Money topic where the JICA Grade 3 textbook carries one — a programme difference, not an error. Printed-page basis: JICA textbook pages cite the book’s own printed folio / contents page; JICA manuals cite the manual’s own printed contents page; SBC guides cite the guide’s printed programme/contents pages (the basis is stated in each book entry below); where a contents extraction was partly two-column-interleaved, the PDF page is given as well and marked “PDF p.”. Counting grain: one item per chapter (pupil textbooks), per unit (teacher’s manuals — the manual unit mirrors the textbook chapter of the same number and is counted once, as teacher evidence), and per named topic/unit in the SBC guides’ own programmes. The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the end-of-book/year “Summary” chapters and units in the JICA textbooks and manuals (review apparatus), and the SBC guides’ front sections, assessment-record templates, glossaries/resources and integrated cross-subject project sections. Pupil-textbook rows are COUNTED normally; every manual and SBC guide row is teacher evidence — named in the countries line and in the rows, but outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 items — 0 pupil-textbook rows and 8 teacher-evidence rows — from Book T18 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 8 (SBC)*), Book T19 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 9 (SBC)*), Book T20 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 10 (SBC)*), Book T21 (*Advanced Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 11 (SBC)*), Book T22 (*General Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 11 (SBC)*).

**Book T18:** *Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 8* (SBC teacher guide, Senior Primary) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum; 8,826,348 bytes, 130 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Printed-page basis: the guide’s Yearly Lesson Overview (printed pp. 9–10), lessons 1–50.

**Book T19:** *Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 9* (SBC teacher guide, Secondary) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum; 11,711,664 bytes, 182 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Printed-page basis: the guide’s “Strands, Units and Topics” table (printed pp. 38–42), with its Teaching Content sections. The guide spells its fourth strand “Statistics and Orobability” in the table — a misprint for Probability, taught corrected. Record fault: this guide’s textbooks.tsv tree path 404s; the file was recovered by searching all release assets and matches books.json on size and pages (flag 5).

**Book T20:** *Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 10* (SBC teacher guide, Secondary) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum; 13,103,025 bytes, 152 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Printed-page basis: the guide’s “Strands, Units, Topics and Suggested Lesson Titles” table (printed pp. 38–40), with its Teaching Content sections.

**Book T21:** *Advanced Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 11* (SBC teacher guide) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum; 24,742,306 bytes, 196 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Printed-page basis: the guide’s “Strands, Units, Topics and Suggested Lesson Titles” table (printed p. 39 onwards), with its Teaching Content sections. The Advanced guide shares the General guide’s Numeracy, Measurement, Trigonometry, Geometry, Factorisation, Equations and Data units, adds a Functions and Graphs unit, and has no Financial Mathematics unit. The Grade 11 guides print “Algebrain” for Algebraic in the Factorisation unit header — a misprint, taught corrected.

**Book T22:** *General Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 11* (SBC teacher guide) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum; 19,035,388 bytes, 178 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Printed-page basis: the guide’s “Strands, Units, Topics and Suggested Lesson Titles” table (printed p. 39 onwards), with its Teaching Content sections. The guide prints “Algebrain” for Algebraic in its Factorisation unit header — a misprint, taught corrected.

## Sweden added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Sweden's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 files, 594 PDF pages**, of which **10 carry mathematics**: the national curriculum Lgr22, 2 SPSM pupil materials, and 7 SPSM teacher guides, notes and support publications. Sweden has **no state pupil textbook series** — textbook approval was abolished in 1991, Skolverket sets the curriculum (Lgr22), and the state producer SPSM (Specialpedagogiska skolmyndigheten) publishes special-needs materials — so the pupil teaching in hand is SPSM material for blind and deaf pupils, and nothing else: 13 pupil items (the Abakus exercise book and the Växelspelet money game), 81 teacher-evidence items and 79 curriculum-evidence items from Lgr22's Matematik kursplan, 173 items in all. The other 3 files in the read set prove on reading to carry no maths at all and are stocked nowhere: *Skrivmaterial för elever som behöver förstärkta linjer och rutor* is blank ruled/grid stationery (copying masters), the *ABC Skriva-Läsa* teacher guide is literacy only, and a generically named *Arbetsblad* worksheet (pharmacy vocabulary) was pulled in as a hidden-maths spot check and cleared negative. The expansion map is wrong on both axes (flag F4): it listed 8 files as 3 student + 5 teacher, but two of its eight are not Maths at all (F1: the Skriva-Läsa guide, filed in books.json under “Matematik; Svenska”, is a literacy guide — its only number-adjacent moment, drawing a numbered slip 2–5 to pick that many pictures (printed p. 6), is classroom apparatus; F3: the Skrivmaterial, counted as a student Maths book, is blank stationery), a third is the wrong kind (F2: *Räkna med mig*, filed as a student book, is a 105-page handbook for teachers of braille-using pupils — all 35 of its items are teacher evidence), and four maths-bearing files were missed by the map entirely — Lgr22 itself (its section 5.5 is the full Matematik syllabus), the *Arbeta med språkstörning i matematikundervisningen* support publication (filed under generic special education), *Växelspelet* (filed under “Verklighetsuppfattning”) and the *Jag handlar* teacher guide (filed under home-economics subjects). Flags F1–F4 are record/map-side: they are reported here and carried in this section; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/sweden.md filing exists. The books' own errors are carried corrected: F5 — both Abakus volumes misprint their print ISBNs with a “987-” prefix (Övningsbok “987-91-28-11602-2”, Handledning “987-91-28-11600-8”; each book's PDF ISBN carries the correct 978- prefix); F6 — *Räkna med mig*'s ISBN is printed malformed as “98-91-28-10386-2”; F7 — the Abakus Handledning's division chapter (printed p. 45) says the pupil must master “multiplikation” where the sense requires division, taught corrected on the division row it touches; F8 — a box in *Räkna med mig* (printed p. 38) misnames the Abakus handbook as “Räkna med mig – handledning”. The placements' record items are stocked nowhere: R1 (Lgr22's assessment criteria, printed pp. 60–63 — they restate the central content as standards and add no content beyond the items), R2 (the Abakus Handledning's exercise sets and maths-card copying masters), R3 (the Arbeta publication's overview material), R4 (*Räkna med mig*'s apparatus), R5 (the Diamanterna card game itself is a physical product, not a book, and is not in hand — only its one-page SPSM note is) and R6 (the *Jag handlar* pupil book is recorded only as a sample that was never staged as a secured file; only its teacher guide is in hand). One basis note: the Övningsbok's multiplication and division exercise pages are abacus diagrams with no printed numerals, so those two placements rest on the book's contents page, section headings and introduction, exactly as the placements state. Sweden's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-heavy)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but 160 of its 173 items are teacher or curriculum evidence and its pupil side is special-needs material only. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items: 5 curriculum evidence, 1 teacher evidence. No Sweden pupil item stocks into this lesson, so Sweden is added to the countries line above and named in the rows below as evidence only, and is NOT added to the acceptance count, on the standing evidence-only precedent.

**Book 1:** *Läroplan för grundskolan, förskoleklassen och fritidshemmet 2022* (Lgr22) (national curriculum) — Sweden, Skolverket, 2024; 1,317,913 bytes, 272 PDF pp., MD5 ea52068ef1b63a71f80885ddc7abdf95. Read for its Matematik kursplan in full (section 5.5, printed pp. 54–63) plus the förskoleklass chapter's maths content; the book's other 18 subject syllabi are out of scope for a Maths read. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — Lgr22 states what must be taught and teaches nothing itself; its rows are worded as the requirements they are and are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure. Its assessment criteria (printed pp. 60–63) restate the central content as standards and are record item R1, stocked nowhere.

**Book 10:** *Räkna med mig* (teacher handbook) — Sweden, SPSM, 2018, best.nr 10386; 20,465,920 bytes, 105 PDF pp., MD5 5fbeac506f239001f466a59aa7574d9e. A 105-page handbook on mathematics teaching for compulsory-school pupils who use braille, addressed to teachers (foreword, printed p. 7) and built on the Lgr11 central content; grabbed from its recorded source_url after the GitHub Contents API returned 404. Filed in the records as a student book — wrong (flag F2): it is not a pupil book, and all 35 of its items are teacher evidence. ## Switzerland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Switzerland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **8 files / 6 works, and no pupil textbook among them**: the three PER brochures *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales*, Cycles 1, 2 and 3 (CIIP, Version 3.0, août 2024), whose Mathématiques parts are placed here; the Ticino *Piano di studio della scuola dell'obbligo ticinese* (DECS, edizione perfezionata 2022), whose Matematica chapter is placed here; the *Lehrplan 21 Graubünden* (Fassung vom 15.03.2016), whose Mathematik plan is placed here — one work in three filed language editions (German, Romansh, Italian), read in full in the German edition, verified identical in structure and pagination in the other two, and cited once; and one teacher guide, *MaMa — Linee guida del progetto MaMa: Ambito Numeri e calcolo* (DECS/CeRDD, 2021), whose didactic rulings are placed here as teacher evidence — 425 items in all (364 curriculum evidence + 61 teacher-guide evidence, 0 pupil). The expansion map's kind split is WRONG (record-level; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records): it recorded "3 student + 1 teacher", but the three "student" files are the PER **curriculum** brochures mislabelled — **Switzerland has 0 pupil Maths textbooks in hand**, and its prescribed pupil series (Schweizer Zahlenbuch, Mathematik Primarstufe / Sekundarstufe I (LMVZ), Mathwelt, Mathbuch, MER Mathématiques 1–11, Ticino's Base Matematica 1–4) are commercial and licence-gated, not in hand and not read. Further maths was found hiding under "all subjects" labels: the Ticino Piano and the Lehrplan 21 Graubünden are all-subjects volumes carrying full mathematics chapters. Every grabbed file was verified SHA-256 byte-exact against its books.json record. Checked in hand and excluded with reasons, stocked nowhere: the three PER *Aperçus des contenus* (one example-grain maths page each — record items R1–R3), the PER *Présentation générale* (framework structure only), the PER *Éducation numérique* brochure (the computer-science curriculum; mathematics appears only as cross-links — a separate school subject) and the four Vaud *<DÉ>CODAGE* digital-education teacher manuals (contents pages of all four read: Science informatique / Médias / Usages only — a separate school subject); inside the PER brochures, the Sciences de la nature and Sciences humaines et sociales parts are other subjects, the *Modélisation* objectives are process apparatus whose content is carried by the per-theme problem-solving items, and the Lexiques are apparatus; inside the Ticino volume, every chapter other than Matematica is another subject and the process/attitude competence targets carry no subject content; inside the Lehrplan 21 volumes, every subject other than Mathematik is out of scope and the Einführung/Gesamtüberblick chapters are structure, not content; MaMa §§1 and 3 are project apparatus, and §2's quoted Ticino targets are placed at the Ticino items, not double-counted (record item R4). One within-document inconsistency is flagged so it is not stocked: the PER Cycle 1 *Aperçus des contenus* claims Cycle 1 pupils "calculate lengths (perimeter of a rectangle), areas, volumes (cube)" — the governing Cycle 1 brochure requires only comparison and measurement with an étalon (items 46–55), and perimeter/area/volume calculation begins in Cycle 2 (items 158–160); the brochure governs, the Aperçu's Cycle 1 sentence is an overstatement, and it is NOT stocked as a Cycle 1 requirement (record item R1). One document error is called out by a source itself and taught corrected: the MaMa guide rules that there is no "dissociative property" of addition — splitting an addend and associating (16 + 19 = 16 + 10 + 9) is the associative property, and textbooks that name it otherwise are wrong (item 378); the guide's other flagged classroom errors are misconceptions it warns against, not errors it commits. No Liechtenstein file is judged here: Liechtenstein teaches from Switzerland's books, but no file is filed under Liechtenstein in any record. Printed-page basis: PER brochures, Ticino Piano and Lehrplan 21 — printed folio = PDF page − 2; MaMa — printed page = PDF page. Switzerland's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but all 425 of its items are curriculum or teacher-guide evidence and no pupil textbook is in hand. Into this lesson the placements stock 7 items (5 curriculum evidence and 2 teacher evidence) from Book 3, Book 4, Book 5 and Book 6. Switzerland is added to the countries line and named in the rows below as evidence only, and is NOT added to the acceptance count, on the standing evidence-only precedent.

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Thailand's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **6 pupil textbooks: the IPST (Institute for the Promotion of Teaching Science and Technology, Ministry of Education) *Additional Mathematics* series, Matthayom 4–6, Books 1–2** — and placed 267 items. The expansion map's count and kind split are right: staging `books.json` holds exactly these 6 entries (all `subject: Mathematics`, all `kind: student`), `textbooks.tsv` carries exactly these 6 Thailand rows across all subjects — so no Maths book hides under another subject label — and `phase3-push-state.json` lists 5 of the 6 as pushed. Each book was re-fetched for the read — from the repo tree (Contents API) or the named release where the pusher had stored it, else from the book's recorded `source_url` (Internet Archive) — verified by byte size against its record, and the grabbed copies were deleted after their placements were written. Printed page = PDF page − 12 in all six books: the front matter runs cover → title → preface → course description → contents (contents on PDF pp. 10–11, PDF p. 12 blank) and the body opens at printed p. 1 on PDF p. 13, verified against printed folios at chapter starts in every book. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching point — a named concept, method, rule or worked skill with its printed page. The placements' named exclusions — exercise sets and end-of-chapter test sets in all six books; worked-example repeats; chapter openers and historical sidebars; front matter; and back matter (bibliography, the bilingual Thai–English glossary appendix and production credits) — are stocked nowhere. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. The read's one flagged claim rides with the set, honestly (flag F1): the `textbooks.tsv` GitHub blob URL for M5 Book 1 is dead — the authenticated Contents API lookup returns 404, the file is absent from `phase3-push-state.json` and the staging copy is gone — but the book itself is fully confirmed, re-fetched from its recorded `source_url` (Internet Archive `math3_text.pdf`) at 6,204,831 bytes, matching `books.json` exactly, so the flag is on the GitHub filing claim only, not on the book, and the library's pushed count of 5 for Thailand is consistent with it. The two pending library rulings do not bite on this set: all six books are official IPST textbooks, so no approved-publisher question arises, and no in-hand file is a flipbook capture or publisher sample — every file is the complete book; the elibrarycub “Sample_ebook” files and AnyFlip candidates in the download list are link-only, not in hand and not read. Thailand COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Thailand book is a pupil textbook. ## Uganda added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Uganda's whole in-hand Maths library from the PDFs — **8 files / 8 works: 7 pupil books + 1 teacher's guide**, all Uganda Ministry of Education and Sports / NCDC *Home-Study Learning* books (August 2020) — and the expansion map's “7 student + 1 teacher, FILES-IN-HAND” count and split are **correct**, with one correction of kind: the Primary Five pupil book is not a standalone Maths book — it is the Mathematics booklet (package PDF pp. 9–166) inside the NCDC Primary Five merged home-study package (Mathematics + Integrated Science + English + Social Studies, 350 pp.). Maths content hiding under an all-subjects label was checked for across the whole in-hand set; this package is the only such volume. The seven pupil books (Primary Four, the Primary Five booklet, Primary Six, Senior One, Senior Two, Senior Three, Senior Four) are real pupil teaching books — lessons, worked examples and exercises addressed to the learner — so Uganda's pupil rows **count** in a lesson's taught-in figure. The one teacher book, the Senior One *Teacher's Guide* (prototype, 76 pp.), is **teacher evidence only** and counts nowhere; it covers all 12 Senior One prototype topics, including five topics (1 Number Bases, 2 Working with Integers, 8 Angle Properties, 9 Data Collection and Presentation, 12 Time and Time Tables) for which no Senior One pupil text is in hand, and its evidence for those topics carries that “no pupil book in hand” caveat. NOT in hand, and cited nowhere: the Senior One prototype pupil Mathematics textbook (NCDC posted only the Teacher's Guide) and the Scribd combined S1&S2 Science Package (no download route). The placements number **324 items: 278 pupil items and 46 teacher-evidence items (TG1–TG46)** — one item per distinct lesson in the home-study books, one per distinct topic treatment in the guide. Page citations are PDF pages for the pupil books (in the primary home-study books the printed folios run 6 behind: printed = PDF − 6; Primary Five pages are the merged package's PDF pages) and the guide's printed pages for the TG items. The books' own errors are taught corrected: the Senior One home-study book heads the conversion example at PDF p. 8 “Convert 13/4 into an improper fraction” — 13/4 is already improper, and the worked example in fact converts it to the mixed number 3¼; the Primary Six book's triangle-angles exercise (PDF p. 85) prints two interior angles as “360 and 540” — the degree signs set as zeros, read as 36° and 54°; the Senior Three book heads its mensuration topic “TRIGONOMETRY” while its own stated outcomes and lessons are units, surface area and volume — taught as mensuration. Uganda's own ways are kept: equations written with a shape for the unknown before letters (Primary Four); integers walked on a ground number line, face forward for positive and back for backward (Primary Five and Six); base five taught in primary school (Primary Five); fractions taught by slicing chapattis (Senior One); income tax worked from Uganda's own tax bands and allowances (Senior Three). Uganda's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every Maths book in hand has been read fresh and placed; what remains outside the set (the Senior One prototype pupil textbook, the Senior Two–Four prototype books and guides) is not in hand and is a library hunting matter, not a course hold. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 pupil item.

## United States added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read the United States' whole in-hand Maths library from the PDFs — **5 pupil textbooks, 0 teacher guides, all OpenStax** (the CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 open-textbook line of Rice University, Houston, Texas) — and the expansion map's “5 student Maths books, 0 teacher” count is **right**. The United States has no national textbook series and no federal textbook: the set is one nonprofit publisher's open-textbook line, adopted course by course across US states and districts, and every book is a genuine pupil textbook — exposition, worked examples and practice addressed to the student — so the United States' rows **count** in a lesson's taught-in figure. Three record-level flags ride with the set; none of them is a course claim. **F1 — the “Grade 13” filing hides the level split:** four of the five books are college textbooks filed at “Grade 13” (*Introductory Statistics 2e*, *Calculus Volume 1*, *College Algebra 2e*, *Algebra and Trigonometry 2e*); only *Algebra 1* is a school book (the US high-school Algebra 1 course, filed Grade 12), and the set is never presented as five school-grade books. **F2 — the push-state is silent but GitHub holds the files:** `phase3-push-state.json` has no United States entry and nothing US sits in phase3-staging, yet all five files were verified live on GitHub at their exact recorded byte sizes and were read from there — the books ARE in hand and no re-push is owed. **F3 — two of the books duplicate each other:** *Algebra and Trigonometry 2e* repeats *College Algebra 2e* section for section in 59 of its 79 sections (its Chapters 1–6 keep the same pagination), so the country's distinct teaching is 299 items of the 358 placed; both books are nevertheless cited for their own in-hand copies. Honest limit, stated plainly: the in-hand coverage is top-end only — one high-school book and four college books; no US elementary or middle-school Maths book is in hand (the Texas Bluebonnet Learning sets and the New York EngageNY/Eureka Math modules in the library records are link-only, and the US set's OpenStax *Principles of Microeconomics for AP Courses 2e* was checked and excluded — an economics textbook, not mathematics). Counting grain: one item per numbered teaching section of a book (*Algebra 1*: one item per numbered lesson; a lesson's sub-activities are its steps, not separate items). Named exclusions, stocked nowhere: chapter introductions, chapter reviews, formula reviews, practice and homework sets, appendices, answer keys and calculator notes in every book; *Introductory Statistics 2e*'s 22 guided lab and experiment sections (record items R1–R22 — a chapter's teaching applied to one data set, not counted); *Algebra 1*'s unit overviews, readiness reviews and wrap-ups and its nine unit projects. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items — 4 from *Introductory Statistics 2e*, 1 from *College Algebra 2e* and 1 from *Algebra and Trigonometry 2e*.

**Book 1:** *Introductory Statistics 2e* (pupil book) — OpenStax, Rice University, Houston, Texas; senior contributing authors Barbara Illowsky and Susan Dean (De Anza College); © 2026 Rice University, CC BY-NC-SA 4.0; 23,503,369 bytes, 847 PDF pp. A college / AP-level pupil textbook, filed at “Grade 13” in the library. Printed page = PDF page − 12 in the body. PUPIL BOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 2:** *College Algebra 2e* (pupil book) — OpenStax, Rice University, Houston, Texas; senior contributing author Jay Abramson (Arizona State University); © 2026 Rice University, CC BY-NC-SA 4.0; 84,220,165 bytes, 1,073 PDF pp. A college pupil textbook, filed at “Grade 13” in the library. Printed pages are the book's own folios. PUPIL BOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 3:** *Algebra and Trigonometry 2e* (pupil book) — OpenStax, Rice University, Houston, Texas; senior contributing author Jay Abramson (Arizona State University); © 2026 Rice University, CC BY-NC-SA 4.0; 116,281,063 bytes, 1,467 PDF pp. A college pupil textbook, filed at “Grade 13” in the library. Its Chapters 1–6 and 11–13 repeat *College Algebra 2e*'s teaching section for section (59 of its 79 sections; Chapters 1–6 keep the same pagination) — it is cited here for its own in-hand copy, and its own new teaching is the 20 trigonometry sections of Chapters 7–10. Printed pages are the book's own folios. PUPIL BOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Probability terminology — sample space, outcomes and events, and probability as a proportion of outcomes | United States — *Introductory Statistics 2e* (pupil book), printed p. 168 |
| Independent events and mutually exclusive events, and how to test for each | United States — *Introductory Statistics 2e* (pupil book), printed p. 172 |
| The two basic rules — the addition rule and the multiplication rule, with complements | United States — *Introductory Statistics 2e* (pupil book), printed p. 179 |
| Tree diagrams and Venn diagrams as probability tools | United States — *Introductory Statistics 2e* (pupil book), printed p. 189 |
| Probability — sample spaces, events, and computing probabilities of compound events | United States — *College Algebra 2e* (pupil book), printed p. 924 |
| Probability | United States — *Algebra and Trigonometry 2e* (pupil book), printed p. 1260 |

United States is added to the countries line and to the acceptance count above — all five books are pupil textbooks, so every United States row in this lesson counts. The United States' class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED**.

## Vanuatu added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Vanuatu's whole in-hand Maths set from the file — exactly 1 book, a teacher guide, and no pupil textbook: *Tija Gaed blong Matematiks Yia 3* (Mathematics Teacher Guide, Year 3, Bislama, MoET 2017). The expansion map is CORRECT for the in-hand set (0 student + 1 teacher = 1); the TSV's other distinct Vanuatu books are the Language & Communication Y2, Living in Community Y2 and Science Y3 teacher guides and the Bislama Spelling Dictionary Y2 — no Maths hides under another subject label and there is no "all subjects" Vanuatu volume in hand. The staging PDF was deleted after pushing (the pusher's normal pattern); the read copy was grabbed from the recorded source via its banked Wayback capture (2019-11-17 `id_` replay of education.gov.vu/docs/textbooks/Matematiks_2017.pdf) and is byte-identical to the record: 11,818,504 bytes, 317 PDF pages, SHA-256 2b7aa33d2ee0a81d9512e4f141c208bfc04a0abdf6d5bf77d58356a31cf22ec6, matching the staging `books.json` hash exactly. Printed folio = PDF page \u2212 1 throughout (verified on the outcomes section, weekly lesson footers and the glossary). Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching topic in the Year 3 column of the guide's Learning Outcomes & Indicators tables (printed pp. 12\u201322), worded from the outcome and its indicators and confirmed against the weekly lessons that teach it (Terms 1\u20133, printed pp. 23\u2013294); items taught in a weekly lesson but stated in no Year 3 outcome are marked (D). Assessment questions, resources lists and vocabulary boxes are apparatus for the counted items, not separate items. Record items R1\u2013R4 are stocked nowhere as teaching items: R1 the trilingual Bislama\u2013English\u2013French glossary of Year 3 mathematics words (printed pp. 313\u2013316) — teacher-reference apparatus; R2 the photocopiable appendix masters (printed pp. 295\u2013312) — they carry no teaching topic not already counted; R3 the programme facts from the introduction (printed pp. 4\u20136 — five strands, ten sub-strands, seven hours of Year 3 mathematics a week under the National Timetable Policy 2013); R4 the Beginning / Year 1 / Year 2 columns of the outcomes tables — those are the earlier years' guides, which are NOT in hand for Maths, and nothing from them is counted. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the introduction apart from R3 (printed pp. 4\u201310 — lesson planning, literacy and inclusive-education guidance, teaching craft only); the Beginning/Year 1/Year 2 progression columns; the weekly vocabulary boxes, resources lists and assessment apparatus; and syllabus/timetable exposition carried as R3 only. Book errors E1\u2013E5 are carried taught corrected where they affect stocking: E1 — printed p. 15 abbreviates millilitres "(mm)" (mm is millimetres; millilitres are mL) and writes litres "(l)"; E2 — the Term 3 Week 3 length-conversion lesson (printed p. 233) is headed with the operations outcome NO.3.V1 though it is measurement; E3 — the money lesson (printed p. 202) cites its outcome as "M3.V1" for MM.3.V1; E4 — the Year 3 probability outcome code is printed "CPD.3.V1" (printed p. 21) against the CDP codes of the other columns; E5 — template slips in the term overviews (printed pp. 23, 210), none affecting content. Set boundary: the ministry `/docs/curriculum/` Maths Student's Books Y3\u2013Y6 (English stream), the French-stream Maths 3e\u20136e Ann\u00e9e, the Mathematics Teacher's Guide (Year 4) and the Kindergarten Guide (2013) are exact-URL candidates only — never downloaded, in no `books.json`, not in the push-state, not in hand and not read. Vanuatu is **TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY** in every lesson: every item is worded as what the guide teaches, Vanuatu is named in the countries line and in the rows below, and it is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Ghana precedent. Vanuatu's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 teacher-evidence item(s) from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Tija Gaed blong Matematiks Yia 3* (Mathematics Teacher Guide, Year 3, Bislama) \u2014 Vanuatu, Ministry of Education and Training (MoET, Ministri blong Edukesen mo Trening, Ripablik blong Vanuatu), 2017, developed with Australian/NZ aid support (Education Support Program); 11,818,504 bytes, 317 PDF pages, SHA-256 2b7aa33d2ee0a81d9512e4f141c208bfc04a0abdf6d5bf77d58356a31cf22ec6. Teacher guide \u2014 TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Printed folio = PDF page \u2212 1. Read in full: outcomes and indicators printed pp. 12\u201322; weekly lessons Term 1 printed pp. 23\u2013124, Term 2 printed pp. 125\u2013209, Term 3 printed pp. 210\u2013294.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |

| Chance in everyday life: identifying, grouping and describing events by their chance of happening, judging whether a game is fair (from the numbers and the players' experience), discussing the chance of events such as a cyclone in a dry season, ordering events by how likely they are, and making predictions \u2014 the Year 3 outcome code is printed \u201cCPD.3.V1\u201d, a transposition of the CDP codes used for the other years (placements Error E4) | Vanuatu \u2014 *Tija Gaed blong Matematiks Yia 3* (teacher guide) \u2014 teacher evidence, printed p. 21 (detailed printed pp. 82, 150, 244) |

## Uruguay added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Uruguay's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **6 books: five pupil WORKBOOKS of the official series *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática* (ANEP · DGEIP · CACEEM) — Inicial, Primero (5th ed. 2021), Segundo, Tercero and Quinto (5th ed. 2021) — plus one teacher book, *Matemática en el Segundo Ciclo — Libro para el Maestro* (Grades 4–6)** — and placed 340 items (304 pupil + 36 teacher-evidence). The expansion map's count is right and its kind label is wrong (flag F1 — record-side, reported here, not fixed by this merge; merges never edit the filings or the records): the five pupil books are activity **workbooks**, not student textbooks. A workbook is a pupil book (the South Africa/Peru pattern): the pupil-workbook items below are **teaching content**, so Uruguay's pupil rows are COUNTED in full. The teacher book's items are **teacher evidence only** — worded as teacher evidence, named in the countries line and in the rows, and added to no acceptance count (the Ghana precedent); the teacher book is also the only in-hand coverage of the Grade 4 and Grade 6 pupil content, whose workbooks are not in hand. **Not in hand, not read, cited nowhere**: the Grade 4 (Cuarto) pupil workbook (36,381,739 B) and the Grade 6 (Sexto) pupil workbook (50,368,322 B) — official DGEIP URLs banked, but every banked Wayback capture is crawler-truncated at 5,242,880 B, the live DGEIP host does not answer from this network, and the Grade 4 book also circulates only as a login-gated Studocu copy (not pursued, per the no-login rule) — and the Primer Ciclo teacher book (Inicial–Grade 3, 5,703,330 B), likewise link-only (captures truncated at 1,048,576 B). Record flag F2 (record-side, reported, not fixed): `textbooks.tsv` carries no row for the Grade 3 workbook, although `phase3-push-state.json` records it as pushed and its staging folder carries a books.json — the book is in hand and was read in full. The Grade 5 workbook is an image-only PDF and was read from page renders; the other five books were read from their text layers. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected: the Grade 3 workbook's garden table (printed p. 79) prints last year's figures 40, 35 and 20 with a total of 85 — the rows sum to 95; the Grade 5 workbook's worked example (printed p. 84) decomposes 0.65 as 6/10 + 9/100, which makes 0.69 — corrected to 6/10 + 5/100 (the decimal form printed beside it, 0.65 = 0.6 + 0.05, is correct); and the Grade 5 book's printed p. 14 side tab names the wrong guide character, "Lechuza" for this book's "Yacaré" — a production error only, content unaffected. (The Grade 5 book's p. 116 "almost two and a half times more casein" line is NOT an error: it is a pupil claim the book itself asks children to judge, and it is placed as such.) The placements' named exclusions are apparatus, stocked nowhere: the workbooks' cut-out pages (recortables), the Inicial personal pages (printed pp. 8–9) and its ice-melting observation page (printed p. 78 — science, no maths content), and the Grade 5 opening questionnaire, guide-character pages and recortables. Printed-page basis: printed page = PDF page − 2 in all six books. Counting grain: one item per distinct activity / teaching point in a workbook; one item per distinct teaching guidance / content statement in the teacher book. Into this lesson the placements stock 24 items — 20 pupil-workbook rows and 4 teacher-evidence rows.

**Book 1:** *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Inicial* (pupil workbook) — Uruguay, ANEP · CEIP/DGEIP · CACEEM, 1st edition, ISBN 978-9974-677-76-0; 7,366,728 bytes, 99 PDF pp., MD5 b7f3dbb92271a6dd4cb89140d6d128f7. PUPIL WORKBOOK — counted: the workbook teaches each topic through activities and guided practice. Printed page = PDF page − 2. Guide character: Lobito.
